# Public-source code in libGLcore

Much of `libGLcore.so` is not board code. The software renderer, the display-list machinery, the pixel paths and the name tables descend from the OpenGL sample implementation that SGI published, which this repository carries as the `ogl-sample` submodule. This chapter gives each such routine of the 6.5.30m N32 library its own entry: what it does, the public source file that holds the same routine, and whether it reaches the board. The board-specific routines are in [libglcore.md](libglcore.md), [functions.md](functions.md) and [opengl.md](opengl.md); the routines of the CRIME-specific directories are left to those chapters.

Public source: the sample implementation under `ogl-sample/gfx/lib/opengl`. Paths in the entries are relative to `ogl-sample`. The shipped source file of a routine is the heading it sits under, for example `so_accumop.c`; the sample file that holds the same routine is named in the entry.

Labels follow [AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md). An entry is **Established** only where the whole routine was read against the public body. **Inferred** entries are matched to the public routine by its name, its direct calls and the extent of its source, and were not read end to end. Each routine has an entry for the N32 library of the IP32CRM32 build (`usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so`). Where the o32 build (`usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM/libGLcore.so`) of the same routine makes the same number of calls and has a comparable size, it has its own entry at its own address, always inferred because only the N32 body was compared with the public source; those entries leave out routines whose description depends on structure offsets or sizes, which differ between the builds. The two builds never share code, so no entry counts for both.

Direct calls that the disassembly labels with the name of a neighbouring symbol are calls to file-local routines.

## Software renderer (soft)

### so_accumop.c

- `Load` in usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so: loads the accumulation buffer from the colour buffer, multiplying each component by a value and storing it in the accumulator's fixed-point cells, and it occupies 1084 bytes at address 0xd9bb2f0; the public sample-implementation source gfx/lib/opengl/glcore/s_accum.c holds the same routine, matched by name, neither the shipped routine nor the public body calling anything, the 68 source lines of the shipped routine being of comparable length to the public body's 76, and it makes no direct access to a board register window. Inferred.
- `Accumulate` in usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so: adds the colour buffer's pixels times a value to the accumulation buffer's cells over the clip rectangle, and it occupies 1304 bytes at address 0xd9bb730; the public sample-implementation source gfx/lib/opengl/glcore/s_accum.c holds the same routine, matched by name, neither the shipped routine nor the public body calling anything, the 68 source lines of the shipped routine being of comparable length to the public body's 76, and it makes no direct access to a board register window. Inferred.
- `Mult` in usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so: multiplies every cell of the accumulation buffer in the clip rectangle by a value, and it occupies 1092 bytes at address 0xd9bbc50; the public sample-implementation source gfx/lib/opengl/glcore/s_accum.c holds the same routine, matched by name, neither the shipped routine nor the public body calling anything, the 60 source lines of the shipped routine being of comparable length to the public body's 72, and it makes no direct access to a board register window. Inferred.
- `Add` in usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so: adds a value, scaled to the fixed-point range, to every component of every cell of the accumulation buffer in the clip rectangle, and it occupies 556 bytes at address 0xd9bc0a0; the public sample-implementation source gfx/lib/opengl/glcore/s_accum.c holds the same routine, matched by name, neither the shipped routine nor the public body calling anything, the 41 source lines of the shipped routine being of comparable length to the public body's 51, and it makes no direct access to a board register window. Inferred.
- `Return` in usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so: writes the accumulation buffer back to the colour buffer, scaling each cell by a value to a colour and storing it over the clip rectangle, and it occupies 396 bytes at address 0xd9bc2d0; the public sample-implementation source gfx/lib/opengl/glcore/s_accum.c holds the same routine, matched by name, neither the shipped routine nor the public body calling anything, the 32 source lines of the shipped routine being of comparable length to the public body's 42, and it makes no direct access to a board register window. Inferred.
- `Clear` in usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so: fills the accumulation buffer's clip rectangle with the clear colour scaled to the buffer's fixed-point range, row by row, and it occupies 812 bytes at address 0xd9bc460; the public sample-implementation source gfx/lib/opengl/glcore/s_accum.c holds the same routine, matched by name, neither the shipped routine nor the public body calling anything, the 49 source lines of the shipped routine being of comparable length to the public body's 52, and it makes no direct access to a board register window. Inferred.
- `Pick` in usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so: does nothing and returns, the accumulation buffer's procedure picker having no state to choose, and it occupies 8 bytes at address 0xd9bc790; the public sample-implementation source gfx/lib/opengl/glcore/s_accum.c holds the same routine, matched by name, neither the shipped routine nor the public body calling anything, the shipped routine spanning 1 source lines against 7 in the public body, and it makes no direct access to a board register window. Inferred.
- `__glInitAccum64` in usr/gfx/arch/IP32CRM32/libGLcore.so: sets up a 64-bit-cell accumulation buffer record by filling in its pick, clear, accumulate, load, return, multiply and add procedures, and it occupies 280 bytes at address 0xd9bc7a0; the public sample-implementation source gfx/lib/opengl/glcore/s_accum.c holds the same routine, matched by name and by its direct calls, including `__glInitBuffer`, the 34 source lines of the shipped routine being of comparable length to the public body's 30, and it makes no direct access to a board register window.
